Title: Vita mirabilis et mors pretiosa venerabilis sororis Rosae de S. Maria Limensis, ex tertio ordine S. P. Dominici
Creator: Leonardus Hansen, O.P., provincial of England and socius of the Master General
Publisher: Romae: typis Nicolai Angeli Tinassii, 1664
Publication Date: 1664
Rights: Public Domain (published 1664, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: lat
Source: Internet Archive item wotb_6743752 (https://archive.org/details/wotb_6743752), the item's own wotb_6743752_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: The Johns Hopkins University Sheridan Libraries; host metadata date: 1654. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 611d51d42a4618507fcf28b2f94e4fab4791dd4dfbecdac3f45ab4c06ca38587.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Johns Hopkins University Sheridan Libraries scan; clean OCR for its age, the long s read as f.
Content note: The Life of Rose of Lima drawn from the Lima process for her beatification, dedicated to Alexander VII: her childhood, the Third Order habit, her penances and hermitage in her father's garden, her mystical life, her examination by theologians, her death in 1617 and the miracles after it.
